Build edge map\n    edge_map = {}\n    for edge in edges:\n        from_id = edge['from']\n        to_name = edge['to']\n\n        if from_id not in edge_map:\n            edge_map[from_id] = []\n        edge_map[from_id].append(to_name)\n\n    # Write edges\n        for edge in edges:\n            from_id = edge['from']\n            to = edge['to']\n            edge_text = edge['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n\n            # Check if target is a node ID or scene name\n            if str(to).isdigit():\n                f.write(f'  {from_id} -> {to} [label=\"{edge_text}\"];\\n')\n            else:\n                # Process the last scene\n    if current_scene and scene_lines:\n        process_scene(current_scene, scene_lines, nodes, edges, node_id_counter)\n\n    # Validate graph\n    validate_graph(nodes, edges)\n\n    return {\n        'nodes': nodes,\n        'edges': edges\n    }\n\n\ndef process_scene(scene_name: str, scene_lines: List[Dict],\n                 nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict],\n                 start_id: int) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[Dict]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a single scene's lines and add to nodes/edges lists.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (new_nodes, new_edges)\n    \"\"\"\n    scene_nodes = []\n    scene_edges = []\n    node_id = start_id\n\n    for line_data in scene_lines:\n        # Create node\n        node = {\n            'id': node_id,\n            'text': line_data['text'],\n            'speaker': line_data['speaker'],\n            'type': line_data['type']\n        }\n        scene_nodes.append(node)\n\n        # Create edge if there's a target\n        if 'target' in line_data and line_data['target']:\n            edge = {\n                'from': node_id,\n                'to': line_data['target'],\n                'text': f\"{line_data['speaker']}: {line_data['text']}\" if line_data['type'] == 'choice' else line_data['text']\n            }\n            scene_edges.append(edge)\n\n        node_id += 1\n\n    # Add to global lists\n    nodes.extend(scene_nodes)\n    edges.extend(scene_edges)\n\n    return scene_nodes, scene_edges\n\n\ndef validate_graph(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate that the graph meets the constraints:\n    1. All nodes must be reachable from the first node\n    2. All edge targets must exist except for \"End\"\n    \"\"\"\n    if not nodes:\n        return\n\n    # Build adjacency list for reachability check\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n    node_name_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}\n    scene_name_map = {}\n\n    # Map scene names to their first node ID\n    # This is a bit tricky - we need to identify which scene name corresponds to which node\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the scene name as a pseudo-node if it's referenced\n\n    # First, identify the starting node (should be the first node in the list)\n    start_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n\n    # Build reachability set\n    reachable = set()\n    to_visit = [start_node_id]\n\n    # Build edge map\n    edge_map = {}\n    for edge in edges:\n        from_id = edge['from']\n        to_name = edge['to']\n\n        if from_id not in edge_map:\n            edge_map[from_id] = []\n        edge_map[from_id].append(to_name)\n\n    # BFS to find all reachable node IDs\n    while to_visit:\n        current = to_visit.pop(0)\n        if current in reachable:\n            continue\n        reachable.add(current)\n\n        # If this node has edges, check targets\n        if current in edge_map:\n            for target_name in edge_map[current]:\n                # Target might be a scene name or an ID\n                # Check if it's an ID first\n                if target_name in node_ids:\n                    if target_name not in reachable:\n                        to_visit.append(target_name)\n                # Otherwise it's a scene name - the target should be handled when that scene is processed\n\n    # Note: Full scene name to node ID mapping would require a second pass\n    # For now, we'll do basic validation\n\n    # Check that all edge targets exist (except \"End\")\n    for edge in edges:\n        target = edge['to']\n        if target != 'End':\n            # Check if target is a node ID in our graph\n            if not any(node['id'] == target for node in nodes):\n                # If not an ID, it should be a scene name\n                # We'll assume scene names are valid targets\n                pass\n\n\ndef generate_dot(graph: Dict, output_file: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate a DOT file for graph visualization.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The parsed graph dictionary\n        output_file: Path to output DOT file\n    \"\"\"\n    nodes = graph['nodes']\n    edges = graph['edges']\n\n    with open(output_file, 'w') as f:\n        f.write('digraph DialogueGraph {\\n')\n        f.write('  rankdir=LR;\\n')\n        f.write('  node [shape=box, style=rounded];\\n\\n')\n\n        # Write nodes\n        for node in nodes:\n            node_id = node['id']\n            speaker = node['speaker'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            text = node['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            node_type = node['type']\n\n            if node_type == 'choice':\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled];\\n')\n            else:\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightgray, style=filled];\\n')\n\n        f.write('\\n')\n\n        # Write edges\n        for edge in edges:\n            from_id = edge['from']\n            to = edge['to']\n            edge_text = edge['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n\n            # Check if target is a node ID or scene name\n            if str(to).isdigit():\n                f.write(f'  {from_id} -> {to} [label=\"{edge_text}\"];\\n')\n            else:\n                # Scene name - create a label node for it\n                scene_label = to.replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n                f.write(f'  {from_id} -> \"{scene_label}\" [label=\"{edge_text}\"];\\n')\n\n        f.write('}\\n')\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main entry point\"\"\"\n    # Read the input script\n    with open('/app/script.txt', 'r') as f:\n        script_text = f.read()\n\n    # Parse the script\n    graph = parse_script(script_text)\n\n    # Write JSON output\n    with open('/app/dialogue.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(graph, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Map scene names to their first node ID\n\n    # First pass: parse scenes and lines\n    for line in lines:\n        line = line.strip()\n        if not line:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for scene header\n        scene_match = re.match(r'\\[([^\\]]+)\\]', line)\n        if scene_match:\n            # Save previous scene's lines\n            if current_scene and scene_lines:\n                scene_nodes, scene_edges = process_scene(\n                    current_scene, scene_lines, nodes, edges, node_id_counter\n                )\n                # Map scene names to their first node ID\n\n    # First pass: parse scenes and lines\n    for line in lines:\n        line = line.strip()\n        if not line:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for scene header\n        scene_match = re.match(r'\\[([^\\]]+)\\]', line)\n        if scene_match:\n            # Save previous scene's lines\n            if current_scene and scene_lines:\n                scene_nodes, scene_edges = process_scene(\n                    current_scene, scene_lines, nodes, edges, node_id_counter\n                )\n                # Map scene to its first node\n                if scene_nodes:\n                    scene_to_first_node[current_scene] = scene_nodes[0]['id']\n                node_id_counter = scene_nodes[-1]['id'] + 1 if scene_nodes else node_id_counter\n\n            # Start new scene\n            current_scene = scene_match.group(1)\n            scene_lines = []\n            continue\n\n        # Check for dialogue/choice line\n        if current_scene and line:\n            # Match pattern: Speaker: text -> NextScene\n            # or: Number. text -> NextScene\n            dialogue_match = re.match(r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\S+)$', line)\n\n            if dialogue_match:\n                speaker = dialogue_match.group(1).strip()\n                content = dialogue_match.group(2).strip()\n                target = dialogue_match.group(3).strip()\n\n                # Check if this is a choice (starts with number)\n                choice_match = re.match(r'^(\\d+)\\.\\s*(.+)$', content)\n                if choice_match:\n                    choice_num = choice_match.group(1)\n                    choice_text = choice_match.group(2)\n                    scene_lines.append({\n                        'type': 'choice',\n                        'speaker': speaker,\n                        'text': choice_text,\n                        'number': choice_num,\n                        'target': target\n                    })\n                else:\n                    scene_lines.append({\n                        'type': 'line',\n                        'speaker': speaker,\n                        'text': content,\n                        'target': target\n                    })\n\n    # Process the last scene\n    if current_scene and scene_lines:\n        scene_nodes, scene_edges = process_scene(current_scene, scene_lines, nodes, edges, node_id_counter)\n        if scene_nodes:\n            scene_to_first_node[current_scene] = scene_nodes[0]['id']\n\n    # Show first 10\n\n    # Validate graph\n    validate_graph(nodes, edges)\n\n    return {\n        'nodes': nodes,\n        'edges': edges\n    }\n\n\ndef process_scene(scene_name: str, scene_lines: List[Dict],\n                 nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict],\n                 start_id: int) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[Dict]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a single scene's lines and add to nodes/edges lists.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (new_nodes, new_edges)\n    \"\"\"\n    scene_nodes = []\n    scene_edges = []\n    node_id = start_id\n\n    for line_data in scene_lines:\n        # Create node\n        node = {\n            'id': node_id,\n            'text': line_data['text'],\n            'speaker': line_data['speaker'],\n            'type': line_data['type']\n        }\n        scene_nodes.append(node)\n\n        # Create edge if there's a target\n        if 'target' in line_data and line_data['target']:\n            edge = {\n                'from': node_id,\n                'to': line_data['target'],\n                'text': f\"{line_data['speaker']}: {line_data['text']}\" if line_data['type'] == 'choice' else line_data['text']\n            }\n            scene_edges.append(edge)\n\n        node_id += 1\n\n    # Add to global lists\n    nodes.extend(scene_nodes)\n    edges.extend(scene_edges)\n\n    return scene_nodes, scene_edges\n\n\ndef convert_edge_targets(edges: List[Dict], scene_to_first_node: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Convert edge targets from scene names to actual node IDs.\n\n    Args:\n        edges: List of edge dictionaries\n        scene_to_first_node: Mapping from scene names to first node IDs\n\n    Returns:\n        List of edges with converted targets\n    \"\"\"\n    converted_edges = []\n    for edge in edges:\n        new_edge = edge.copy()\n        target = edge['to']\n\n        # Convert scene name to node ID if it exists in the mapping\n        if target in scene_to_first_node:\n            new_edge['to'] = scene_to_first_node[target]\n        # Otherwise keep the original target (e.g., \"End\" or unknown scene)\n\n        converted_edges.append(new_edge)\n\n    return converted_edges\n\n\ndef validate_graph(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate that the graph meets the constraints:\n    1. All nodes must be reachable from the first node\n    2. All edge targets must exist except for \"End\"\n    \"\"\"\n    if not nodes:\n        return\n\n    # Build adjacency list for reachability check\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n    node_name_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}\n    scene_name_map = {}\n\n    # Map scene names to their first node ID\n    # This is a bit tricky - we need to identify which scene name corresponds to which node\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the scene name as a pseudo-node if it's referenced\n\n    # First, identify the starting node (should be the first node in the list)\n    start_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n\n    # Build reachability set\n    reachable = set()\n    to_visit = [start_node_id]\n\n    # Build edge map\n    edge_map = {}\n    for edge in edges:\n        from_id = edge['from']\n        to_name = edge['to']\n\n        if from_id not in edge_map:\n            edge_map[from_id] = []\n        edge_map[from_id].append(to_name)\n\n    # BFS to find all reachable node IDs\n    while to_visit:\n        current = to_visit.pop(0)\n        if current in reachable:\n            continue\n        reachable.add(current)\n\n        # If this node has edges, check targets\n        if current in edge_map:\n            for target_name in edge_map[current]:\n                # Target might be a scene name or an ID\n                # Check if it's an ID first\n                if target_name in node_ids:\n                    if target_name not in reachable:\n                        to_visit.append(target_name)\n                # Otherwise it's a scene name - the target should be handled when that scene is processed\n\n    # Note: Full scene name to node ID mapping would require a second pass\n    # For now, we'll do basic validation\n\n    # Check that all edge targets exist (except \"End\")\n    for edge in edges:\n        target = edge['to']\n        if target != 'End':\n            # Check if target is a node ID in our graph\n            if not any(node['id'] == target for node in nodes):\n                # If not an ID, it should be a scene name\n                # We'll assume scene names are valid targets\n                pass\n\n\ndef generate_dot(graph: Dict, output_file: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate a DOT file for graph visualization.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The parsed graph dictionary\n        output_file: Path to output DOT file\n    \"\"\"\n    nodes = graph['nodes']\n    edges = graph['edges']\n\n    with open(output_file, 'w') as f:\n        f.write('digraph DialogueGraph {\\n')\n        f.write('  rankdir=LR;\\n')\n        f.write('  node [shape=box, style=rounded];\\n\\n')\n\n        # Write nodes\n        for node in nodes:\n            node_id = node['id']\n            speaker = node['speaker'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            text = node['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            node_type = node['type']\n\n            if node_type == 'choice':\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled];\\n')\n            else:\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightgray, style=filled];\\n')\n\n        f.write('\\n')\n\n        # Write edges\n        for edge in edges:\n            from_id = edge['from']\n            to = edge['to']\n            edge_text = edge['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n\n            # Check if target is a node ID or scene name\n            if str(to).isdigit():\n                f.write(f'  {from_id} -> {to} [label=\"{edge_text}\"];\\n')\n            else:\n                # Save previous scene's lines\n            if current_scene and scene_lines:\n                scene_nodes, scene_edges = process_scene(\n                    current_scene, scene_lines, nodes, edges, node_id_counter\n                )\n                # Match pattern: Speaker: text -> NextScene\n            # or: Number. text -> NextScene\n            dialogue_match = re.match(r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\S+)$', line)\n\n            if dialogue_match:\n                speaker = dialogue_match.group(1).strip()\n                content = dialogue_match.group(2).strip()\n                target = dialogue_match.group(3).strip()\n\n                # If not an ID, it should be a scene name\n                # We'll assume scene names are valid targets\n                pass\n\n\ndef generate_dot(graph: Dict, output_file: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate a DOT file for graph visualization.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The parsed graph dictionary\n        output_file: Path to output DOT file\n    \"\"\"\n    nodes = graph['nodes']\n    edges = graph['edges']\n\n    with open(output_file, 'w') as f:\n        f.write('digraph DialogueGraph {\\n')\n        f.write('  rankdir=LR;\\n')\n        f.write('  node [shape=box, style=rounded];\\n\\n')\n\n        # Write nodes\n        for node in nodes:\n            node_id = node['id']\n            speaker = node['speaker'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            text = node['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            node_type = node['type']\n\n            if node_type == 'choice':\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled];\\n')\n            else:\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightgray, style=filled];\\n')\n\n        f.write('\\n')\n\n        # All edge targets must exist except for \"End\"\n    \"\"\"\n    if not nodes:\n        return\n\n    # Build adjacency list for reachability check\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n    node_name_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}\n    scene_name_map = {}\n\n    # If not an ID, it should be a scene name\n                # We'll assume scene names are valid targets\n                pass\n\n\ndef generate_dot(graph: Dict, output_file: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate a DOT file for graph visualization.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The parsed graph dictionary\n        output_file: Path to output DOT file\n    \"\"\"\n    nodes = graph['nodes']\n    edges = graph['edges']\n\n    with open(output_file, 'w') as f:\n        f.write('digraph DialogueGraph {\\n')\n        f.write('  rankdir=LR;\\n')\n        f.write('  node [shape=box, style=rounded];\\n\\n')\n\n        # Write nodes\n        for node in nodes:\n            node_id = node['id']\n            speaker = node['speaker'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            text = node['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            node_type = node['type']\n\n            if node_type == 'choice':\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled];\\n')\n            else:\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightgray, style=filled];\\n')\n\n        f.write('\\n')\n\n        # Write edges\n        for edge in edges:\n            from_id = edge['from']\n            to = edge['to']\n            edge_text = edge['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n\n            # Check if target is a node ID or scene name\n            if str(to).isdigit():\n                f.write(f'  {from_id} -> {to} [label=\"{edge_text}\"];\\n')\n            else:\n                # Create edge if there's a target\n        if 'target' in line_data and line_data['target']:\n            edge = {\n                'from': node_id,\n                'to': line_data['target'],\n                'text': f\"{line_data['speaker']}: {line_data['text']}\" if line_data['type'] == 'choice' else line_data['text']\n            }\n            scene_edges.append(edge)\n\n        node_id += 1\n\n    # Add to global lists\n    nodes.extend(scene_nodes)\n    edges.extend(scene_edges)\n\n    return scene_nodes, scene_edges\n\n\ndef convert_edge_targets(edges: List[Dict], scene_to_first_node: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Convert edge targets from scene names to actual node IDs.\n\n    Args:\n        edges: List of edge dictionaries\n        scene_to_first_node: Mapping from scene names to first node IDs\n\n    Returns:\n        List of edges with converted targets\n    \"\"\"\n    converted_edges = []\n    for edge in edges:\n        new_edge = edge.copy()\n        target = edge['to']\n\n        # All nodes must be reachable from the first node\n    2. All edge targets must exist except for \"End\"\n    \"\"\"\n    if not nodes:\n        return\n\n    # Build adjacency list for reachability check\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n    node_name_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}\n    scene_name_map = {}\n\n    # Map scene names to their first node ID\n    # This is a bit tricky - we need to identify which scene name corresponds to which node\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the scene name as a pseudo-node if it's referenced\n\n    # First, identify the starting node (should be the first node in the list)\n    start_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n\n    # Build reachability set\n    reachable = set()\n    to_visit = [start_node_id]\n\n    # Build edge map\n    edge_map = {}\n    for edge in edges:\n        from_id = edge['from']\n        to_name = edge['to']\n\n        if from_id not in edge_map:\n            edge_map[from_id] = []\n        edge_map[from_id].append(to_name)\n\n    # BFS to find all reachable node IDs\n    while to_visit:\n        current = to_visit.pop(0)\n        if current in reachable:\n            continue\n        reachable.add(current)\n\n        # If this node has edges, check targets\n        if current in edge_map:\n            for target_name in edge_map[current]:\n                # Target might be a scene name or an ID\n                # Check if it's an ID first\n                if target_name in node_ids:\n                    if target_name not in reachable:\n                        to_visit.append(target_name)\n                # Otherwise it's a scene name - the target should be handled when that scene is processed\n\n    # Note: Full scene name to node ID mapping would require a second pass\n    # For now, we'll do basic validation\n\n    # Check that all edge targets exist (except \"End\")\n    for edge in edges:\n        target = edge['to']\n        if target != 'End':\n            # Check if target is a node ID in our graph\n            if not any(node['id'] == target for node in nodes):\n                # If not an ID, it should be a scene name\n                # We'll assume scene names are valid targets\n                pass\n\n\ndef generate_dot(graph: Dict, output_file: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate a DOT file for graph visualization.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The parsed graph dictionary\n        output_file: Path to output DOT file\n    \"\"\"\n    nodes = graph['nodes']\n    edges = graph['edges']\n\n    with open(output_file, 'w') as f:\n        f.write('digraph DialogueGraph {\\n')\n        f.write('  rankdir=LR;\\n')\n        f.write('  node [shape=box, style=rounded];\\n\\n')\n\n        # Write nodes\n        for node in nodes:\n            node_id = node['id']\n            speaker = node['speaker'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            text = node['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n            node_type = node['type']\n\n            if node_type == 'choice':\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled];\\n')\n            else:\n                f.write(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{speaker}\\\\n{text}\", fillcolor=lightgray, style=filled];\\n')\n\n        f.write('\\n')\n\n        # Write edges\n        for edge in edges:\n            from_id = edge['from']\n            to = edge['to']\n            edge_text = edge['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n\n            # Check if target is a node ID or scene name\n            if str(to).isdigit():\n                f.write(f'  {from_id} -> {to} [label=\"{edge_text}\"];\\n')\n            else:\n                # Scene name - create a label node for it\n                scene_label = to.replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n                f.write(f'  {from_id} -> \"{scene_label}\" [label=\"{edge_text}\"];\\n')\n\n        f.write('}\\n')\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main entry point\"\"\"\n    # Map scene names to their first node ID\n\n    # First pass: parse scenes and lines\n    for line in lines:\n        line = line.strip()\n        if not line:\n            continue\n\n        # Check for scene header\n        scene_match = re.match(r'\\[([^\\]]+)\\]', line)\n        if scene_match:\n            # Save previous scene's lines\n            if current_scene and scene_lines:\n                scene_nodes, scene_edges = process_scene(\n                    current_scene, scene_lines, nodes, edges, node_id_counter\n                )\n                # Map scene to its first node\n                if scene_nodes:\n                    scene_to_first_node[current_scene] = scene_nodes[0]['id']\n                node_id_counter = scene_nodes[-1]['id'] + 1 if scene_nodes else node_id_counter\n\n            # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_function_63ossw8x82ws_1","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→#!/usr/bin/env python3\n     2→\"\"\"\n     3→Dialogue Parser - Converts text script to structured JSON graph\n     4→\"\"\"\n     5→\n     6→import re\n     7→import json\n     8→from typing import List, Dict, Set, Tuple\n     9→\n    10→\n    11→def parse_script(text: str) -> Dict:\n    12→    \"\"\"\n    13→    Parse a dialogue script into a structured graph.\n    14→\n    15→    Args:\n    16→        text: The script text to parse\n    17→\n    18→    Returns:\n    19→        Dictionary with 'nodes' and 'edges' keys\n    20→    \"\"\"\n    21→    lines = text.strip().split('\\n')\n    22→    nodes = []\n    23→    edges = []\n    24→    current_scene = None\n    25→    scene_lines = []\n    26→    node_id_counter = 0\n    27→    scene_to_nodes = {}  # Check if this is a choice (starts with number)\n    64→                choice_match = re.match(r'^(\\d+)\\.\\s*(.+)$', content)\n    65→                if choice_match:\n    66→                    choice_num = choice_match.group(1)\n    67→                    choice_text = choice_match.group(2)\n    68→                    scene_lines.append({\n    69→                        'type': 'choice',\n    70→                        'speaker': speaker,\n    71→                        'text': choice_text,\n    72→                        'number': choice_num,\n    73→                        'target': target\n    74→                    })\n    75→                else:\n    76→                    scene_lines.append({\n    77→                        'type': 'line',\n    78→                        'speaker': speaker,\n    79→                        'text': content,\n    80→                        'target': target\n    81→                    })\n    82→            else:\n    83→                # Second try: Match pattern \"Number. text -> NextScene\" (choices without speaker)\n    84→                choice_match = re.match(r'^(\\d+)\\.\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\S+)$', line)\n    85→                if choice_match:\n    86→                    choice_num = choice_match.group(1)\n    87→                    choice_text = choice_match.group(2).strip()\n    88→                    target = choice_match.group(3).strip()\n    89→                    scene_lines.append({\n    90→                        'type': 'choice',\n    91→                        'speaker': '',  # Validate graph\n   110→    validate_graph(nodes, edges)\n   111→\n   112→    return {\n   113→        'nodes': nodes,\n   114→        'edges': edges\n   115→    }\n   116→\n   117→\n   118→def process_scene(scene_name: str, scene_lines: List[Dict],\n   119→                 nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict],\n   120→                 start_id: int) -> Tuple[List[Dict], List[Dict]]:\n   121→    \"\"\"\n   122→    Process a single scene's lines and add to nodes/edges lists.\n   123→\n   124→    Returns:\n   125→        Tuple of (new_nodes, new_edges)\n   126→    \"\"\"\n   127→    scene_nodes = []\n   128→    scene_edges = []\n   129→    node_id = start_id\n   130→\n   131→    for line_data in scene_lines:\n   132→        # Add to global lists\n   153→    nodes.extend(scene_nodes)\n   154→    edges.extend(scene_edges)\n   155→\n   156→    # Store the scene's first node ID globally for later use\n   157→    # This is handled by the caller via scene_to_first_node\n   158→\n   159→    return scene_nodes, scene_edges\n   160→\n   161→\n   162→def convert_edge_targets(edges: List[Dict], scene_to_nodes: Dict) -> List[Dict]:\n   163→    \"\"\"\n   164→    Convert edge targets from scene names to actual node IDs.\n   165→\n   166→    For choice scenes, we need to create multiple edges - one for each choice node.\n   167→\n   168→    Args:\n   169→        edges: List of edge dictionaries\n   170→        scene_to_nodes: Mapping from scene names to list of node IDs in that scene\n   171→\n   172→    Returns:\n   173→        List of edges with converted targets\n   174→    \"\"\"\n   175→    converted_edges = []\n   176→    processed_edges = {}  #
